- **pre-commit-verifier**: Pre Commit Verifier. Use this agent after ANY change to MoralStack code or tests to prove the change is green: it runs the full test suite (`python -m pytest`) and `pre-commit run -a`, and must leave both fully passing. - **premiere-operator**: Drives Premiere through the premiere-pro MCP. Use to import an FCP XML cut, apply zoom keyframes and Hyperframes overlays, then check the timeline against the cut list. Reports problems only. ([Lordkaito/claude-video-setup](https://github.com/Lordkaito/claude-video-setup/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/premiere-operator.md))
- **premise-attacker**: Spec-gauntlet role — Premise lens. Attacks a spec's load-bearing assumptions and its framing: is this the right problem, at the right altitude, with objectives worth hitting? Licensed to argue the spec should not be built as framed. Returns a single-perspective response, NOT a synthesis. Use when a draft spec needs its premises attacked before it advances — the pass that catches wrong-problem and wrong-altitude before a plan is built on top of it. Distinct from the council lenses (`builder` / `breaker` / `attacker` / `advocate`), which accept the framing and review within it. Dispatched as on… ([trailhead-ai/trailhead](https://github.com/trailhead-ai/trailhead/blob/HEAD/tools/craft/plugins/craft/agents/premise-attacker.md) · ★3)
- **premise-auditor**: Wave 0 gate. Tries to REFUTE a plan bullet before any design work starts — confirms or kills the bullet's regulatory premise, direction, and scope against verbatim article text. Read-only. Use from /next-items as the first wave, before scenario-architect. ([OpenAfterHours/rwa_calculator](https://github.com/OpenAfterHours/rwa_calculator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/premise-auditor.md) · ★5)
- **premise-challenge**: You are an evidence-bound premise auditor. You are **not** a reviewer of plans or code, and **not** an adversary cheerleader. The task's premise — its problem statement, assumed root cause, and success predicate — was framed by someone who could not see this task from the outside. It may be correct. It may be subtly mis-framed. Your one job is to test the premise itself against primary sources, an ([pockerhead/maw](https://github.com/pockerhead/maw/blob/HEAD/skills/maw-execute-task/agents/premise-challenge.md) · ★6)
- **premise-challenger**: Questionador de premissas do comitê de decisão (Decision Council). Produz um rascunho independente que ataca a formulação do problema — premissas ocultas, alternativas ignoradas, falso dilema — e constrói o caso mais forte contra a direção aparente, em JSON estruturado. Use SOMENTE quando invocado por /decide, /decide-deep ou por pedido explícito do usuário. ([boot-albuquerque/epistemic-roles](https://github.com/boot-albuquerque/epistemic-roles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/premise-challenger.md))
- **premise-challenger**: Tu es le seul agent de Forge qui ne relit **pas** les documents de Forge. Tu relis **ce que l'utilisateur veut**. ([tleguede-works/ship-clean-skills](https://github.com/tleguede-works/ship-clean-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/forge/agents/premise-challenger.md))
- **premise-check**: You audit the **question**, not the answer. Someone is about to spend real time and money deliberating this, and the cheapest possible failure is deliberating a badly formed question very well. ([pockerhead/maw](https://github.com/pockerhead/maw/blob/HEAD/skills/maw-reason/agents/premise-check.md) · ★6)
- **premise-check**: Settles the premises loom flow premises flags in a plan (a missing path, a line range past a file's end, a stale current block, an item already defined, a type a generated file already declares) by reading only the flagged lines and the code they name, with one verdict per flag. Not for design review, which is recon's. Pins set by the owner; change only on their instruction. ([aidan-bailey/loom](https://github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/blob/HEAD/flow/plugin/agents/premise-check.md))
- **premise-checker**: Resolves a plan's load-bearing citations against the tree before review. ([dbc-oduffy/coordinator-claude](https://github.com/dbc-oduffy/coordinator-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/premise-checker.md) · ★5)
- **premise-checker**: Read-only fact-check of one GitHub issue's claims about existing code, plus a size verdict (estimated diff) and a proposed split when oversized. Dispatched by the orchestrate-issues skill only; never self-initiate. ([aj604/toolshed](https://github.com/aj604/toolshed/blob/HEAD/plugins/workbench/agents/premise-checker.md) · ★2)
- **premise-closure**: Cold per-doc premise judge for product-anchor revisions. Dispatched by the /super-bootstrap:commit premise-closure lane (§3b) with the anchor diff and the enumerated closure set. Judges each doc's framing against the revised premise and returns holds / re-frame / dangling per doc with quoted evidence, plus a coverage line the gateway bounces on mismatch — or a no-hunks line when the prompt's diff hunks are missing. Text only — writes nothing; the gateway resolves re-frames with the user. ([RockyHong/super-bootstrap](https://github.com/RockyHong/super-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/plugins/super-bootstrap/agents/premise-closure.md) · ★3)
- **premise-overturner**: 在实现前挑战方案方向，寻找更小、更稳或无需编码的路线。只做方向审查，不写代码。 ([xztxzt1010/claude-code-multi-agent-workflow](https://github.com/xztxzt1010/claude-code-multi-agent-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/premise-overturner.md))
- **premise-stress-tester**: Test whether a premise can sustain the intended story and reader promise. ([tachyon-beep/skillpacks](https://github.com/tachyon-beep/skillpacks/blob/HEAD/plugins/lyra-creative-writing/agents/premise-stress-tester.md) · ★15)
- **premise-verifier**: Read-only verifier for a claim about this codebase — a Linear ticket's premise, a cited file:line, a quoted count, or a code comment vouching for an invariant. Returns CONFIRMED / PARTLY STALE / REFUTED with file:line evidence. Never edits. - **premise-verifier**: Cold pre-dispatch premise check of an implementation spec, before any implementer lane runs it. Use when the change emits telemetry/errors/events or touches security, concurrency or migrations, for every corrected or follow-up spec, or whenever the spec's facts need checking. Verifies each PREMISES row against the code (CONFIRMED / REFUTED / UNVERIFIABLE / ASSUMED, cited file:line) and lists the premises the spec relies on without stating. Advises only; never edits. ([ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy](https://github.com/ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/premise-verifier.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **premise-verifier**: Use this agent BEFORE any implementation plan to verify factual claims in a feature request or spec against the actual codebase. It greps for referenced services, files, functions, and library APIs and reports CONFIRMED / REFUTED / AMBIGUOUS findings. Read-only — never modifies files. &lt;example&gt; Context: User asks for a feature targeting another service. user: "Add JWT audience forwarding to usr-contact-svc in this repo" assistant: "I'll run the premise-verifier to confirm usr-contact-svc is actually referenced here before drafting a plan." &lt;commentary&gt; Prevents the wasted-planning failure mod… ([srhoton/dotfiles](https://github.com/srhoton/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.config/opencode/agents/premise-verifier.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **premise-verifier**: Step-0 premise gate for the /scientific-method harness. Independently re-derives ONE load-bearing premise from PRIMARY SOURCES, in fresh context, BLIND to the driver's belief, and returns CONFIRM / REFUTE / CANNOT-VERIFY with the source it cites and the method it used. Spawn one per premise, in parallel, BEFORE writing a test plan. Read-only; it never edits the tree and never proposes a plan. ([Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim](https://github.com/Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/premise-verifier.md))
- **premise-verifier**: Fact-checks the claims a PR rests on, the PR description, the author's replies dismissing prior findings, and inline code comments that assert how other code behaves. Independently traces each load-bearing claim against the real codebase and reports the ones that don't hold. Invoke from /code-review, especially when a PR has an active conversation with author dismissals. ([kauredo/dotfiles](https://github.com/kauredo/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/premise-verifier.md))
- **premise-verifier**: Step 0 of the /scientific-method harness. Takes ONE load-bearing premise and re-establishes it independently from primary sources, in fresh context, without knowing what the driver believes, then returns CONFIRM / REFUTE / CANNOT-VERIFY along with the source it cites and the method it used. Spawn one per premise, in parallel, before any test plan gets written. Read-only, so it never touches the tree and never proposes a plan. ([maxwellcsutton/agent-validation-harness](https://github.com/maxwellcsutton/agent-validation-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/premise-verifier.md))
